# de-income-tax: successor figure and rule ledger

Guide SHA256 `afcb21f5552d8db8463ed4d9c7ce8782227dc3c6fd573bb77bc673af4cd596c6`; 27906 bytes; actual scoped writer reads 2026-10-29. Predecessor Guide `2f4e335bbdaa39f04d46874ae1d114460d8f8b5da55238ef2555071b9c7d47dd` and immutable original full ledger at `germany-2026-evidence/views/577d735e0609a07b30dc-de-income-tax.md`. This ledger reuses exact original selected quotations for unchanged claims, with attribution; own selected new reads and administrative/law distinctions are explicit in [source-quotes.md](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md). Writer successor, pending different independent review.

Every original current unit is retained/rebound below; new ordinary corrections add their own units. Original topic maps remain complete in `../support/de-income-tax/old-topic-destinations.json`, alongside the exact native line map. No earlier review verdict is inferred to cover changed bytes.

| ID | Section / native LF | Exact current wording | Sources / classification |
|---|---|---|---|
| 001 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF17–17 | This skill takes one self-employed person's business transactions for 2026, sorts them into the cash-basis profit statement (Einnahmen-Überschuss-Rechnung, EÜR), and works out the income tax, solidarity surcharge and church tax. It is for the 2026 return, filed in 2027. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 002 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF19–19 | See also, and do not repeat here: | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 003 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF21–21 | - **de-einkommensteuer-freelancer**: the full method for Freiberufler and traders (EÜR or books, losses, the 30% and 25% flat expense rates for writers and teachers, 2025 figures for returns filed now). | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 004 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF22–22 | - **de-trade-tax**: trade tax (Gewerbesteuer), add-backs, the municipal multiplier. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 005 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF23–23 | - **de-estimated-tax**: planning prepayments. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 006 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF24–24 | - **de-employee-income-tax**: wages, Werbungskosten, child allowances against Kindergeld, extraordinary burdens. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 007 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF25–25 | - **de-non-resident-tax**: a person who does not live in Germany but has German business income. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 008 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF26–26 | - **de-social-contributions**: contribution rates for the self-employed. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 009 | Germany: income tax for the self-employed, from bank statement to tax, tax year 2026; LF27–27 | - **germany-bookkeeping** and **germany-vat-return**: records, asset pools and VAT returns. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 010 | Who this applies to; LF31–31 | - **In scope.** An individual with a residence or habitual abode in Germany, taxed on worldwide income (§ 1(1) EStG), who earns profit from self-employed work (§ 18 EStG, Anlage S) or a trade (§ 15 EStG, Anlage G), and works out profit by the cash method (§ 4(3) EStG). Single or jointly assessed with a spouse. | [de-income-tax.RES](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-res), [de-income-tax.PROF](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-prof), [de-income-tax.TRADE](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-trade), [de-income-tax.CASH](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-cash), [de-income-tax.SPLIT](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-split). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 011 | Who this applies to; LF32–32 | - **Freiberufler or trade.** Only a trade pays trade tax and gets the § 35 credit. The named professions in § 18(1) no. 1 EStG (doctors, lawyers, engineers, architects, tax advisers, journalists, translators and similar) and scientific, artistic, writing and teaching work are Freiberufler. Everything else run for profit is a trade (§ 15(2) EStG). Unclear cases: refer (see de-einkommensteuer-freelancer). | [de-income-tax.PROF](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-prof), [de-income-tax.TRADE](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-trade), [de-income-tax.CREDIT](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-credit). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 012 | Who this applies to; LF33–33 | - **Cash method or books.** A trader must keep books once the tax office gives notice after turnover over EUR 800,000 or trade profit over EUR 80,000 in a year (§ 141 AO). A Freiberufler can always use the cash method. Books are out of scope here. | [de-income-tax.BOOKS](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-books), [de-income-tax.CASH](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-cash). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 013 | Who this applies to; LF34–34 | - **Who must file.** Without wages, anyone whose total income is above the basic allowance (twice it for spouses) (§ 56 EStDV). With business income the return and the EÜR must be sent electronically (§ 25(4) EStG, § 60(4) EStDV). | [de-income-tax.FILEDUTY](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-fileduty), [de-income-tax.EFILE](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-efile), [de-income-tax.EEUR](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-eeur). Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |
| 014 | Who this applies to; LF35–35 | - **Out of scope.** Companies (GmbH, UG): de-corporate-tax. Partnerships (GbR, OHG, KG, PartG), whose profit is fixed at partnership level. Non-residents with German business income: de-non-resident-tax. Selling or closing the business, extraordinary income (§ 34 EStG), retained profits (§ 34a EStG), foreign income and treaties. | Routing / presentation / express reservation / protected attribution, no new positive legal rule.. Exact original current unit; only native LF rebound |

| 065 | The taxable base; LF96–100 | **Cars.** A car used more than 50% for business can be a business asset: all costs are expenses and private use is added back at 1% of the gross list price per month (§ 6(1) no. 4 EStG), or by logbook. A private car used for business trips: EUR 0.30 per km or the actual cost share. Home-to-premises travel uses the commuting allowance and, for a business car, the additional adjustment below. Electric and hybrid cars have special rules: refer.<br><br>**Business-car commuting adjustment, ordinary combustion car.** In addition to the separate private-use add-back, § 4(5) no. 6 EStG disallows `max(0, D − A)` for travel between home and the business premises. Under the list-price method, `D = 0.0003 × domestic list price at first registration × full one-way kilometres × calendar months`. `A` is the statutory commuting allowance: for this ordinary car case, `EUR 0.38 × full one-way kilometres × actual commuting days`, applying the § 9(1) no. 4 distance and benefit-reduction conditions. When private use is valued under § 6(1) no. 4 sentence 1 or 3, including the logbook method, use the actual documented costs of these commuting trips as `D` instead. Electric/hybrid reductions and family-home trips need their separate rules.<br><br>For a stipulated ordinary car with a EUR 40,000 list price, 20 full km, 12 months and 200 commuting days, `D = 2,880`, `A = 1,520`, and the extra nondeductible amount is EUR 1,360. With actual/logbook commuting costs of EUR 1,000 the extra amount is zero; with EUR 2,000 it is EUR 480. These amounts are separate from the private-use add-back. | [de-income-tax.CAR1](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-car1), [de-income-tax.CARLOG](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-carlog), [de-income-tax.TRAVEL](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-travel), [de-income-tax.COMMUTE](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-commute), [de-income-tax.COMMUTEBRIDGE](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-commutebridge), [R01.business-car](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#r01-business-car), [R01.allowance](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#r01-allowance). Bounded DE-R01–08 wording/evidence successor |

The employee flat rate from the wage tax guidelines is not a rule for the self-employed. | [de-income-tax.BUSCOST](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-buscost), [de-income-tax.PERSONAL](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-personal). Less extraordinary burdens and child allowances (see de-employee-income-tax). Round down to whole euros. | [de-income-tax.BASE](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-base), [de-income-tax.PENSIONCAP](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-pensioncap), [de-income-tax.HEALTH](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-health), [de-income-tax.HEALTHCAP](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-healthcap), [de-income-tax.PAIDCHURCH](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-paidchurch), [de-income-tax.LUMPSUM](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-lumpsum), [de-income-tax.TARIFF](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-tariff). | 069 | The taxable base; LF108–108 | **Step 3: tax.** Apply the tariff (or splitting). Subtract prepayments paid. | [de-income-tax.TARIFF](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-tariff), [de-income-tax.SPLIT](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-split), [de-income-tax.CREDIT](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-credit), [de-income-tax.SoliRate](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-solirate), [de-income-tax.SoliBase](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-solibase), [de-income-tax.SOLILIMIT](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-solilimit), [de-income-tax.CHURCHBASE](germany-2026-evidence/views/794a6e823587146574d0-source-quotes.md#de-income-tax-churchbase). | 084 | Cross-border points; LF134–134 | - **Foreign tax, foreign establishment, moving in or out.** Foreign tax credits are in de-non-resident-tax (§ 34c EStG). If a treaty exempts profit of a foreign establishment, test progression separately under § 32b EStG, including its statutory exceptions; exemption does not itself establish progression. Refer for that treaty and progression assessment. | 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF31 | - **In scope.** An individual with a residence or habitual abode in Germany, taxed on worldwide income (§ 1(1) EStG), who earns profit from self-employed work (§ 18 EStG, Anlage S) or a trade (§ 15 EStG, Anlage G), and works out profit by the cash method (§ 4(3) EStG). Single or jointly assessed with a spouse. | Exact wording retained; current LF31, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF32 | - **Freiberufler or trade.** Only a trade pays trade tax and gets the § 35 credit. The named professions in § 18(1) no. 1 EStG (doctors, lawyers, engineers, architects, tax advisers, journalists, translators and similar) and scientific, artistic, writing and teaching work are Freiberufler. Everything else run for profit is a trade (§ 15(2) EStG). Unclear cases: refer (see de-einkommensteuer-freelancer). | Exact wording retained; current LF32, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF33 | - **Cash method or books.** A trader must keep books once the tax office gives notice after turnover over EUR 800,000 or trade profit over EUR 80,000 in a year (§ 141 AO). A Freiberufler can always use the cash method. Books are out of scope here. | Exact wording retained; current LF33, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF34 | - **Who must file.** Without wages, anyone whose total income is above the basic allowance (twice it for spouses) (§ 56 EStDV). With business income the return and the EÜR must be sent electronically (§ 25(4) EStG, § 60(4) EStDV). | Exact wording retained; current LF34, Who this applies to  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF35 | - **Out of scope.** Companies (GmbH, UG): de-corporate-tax. Partnerships (GbR, OHG, KG, PartG), whose profit is fixed at partnership level. Non-residents with German business income: de-non-resident-tax. Selling or closing the business, extraordinary income (§ 34 EStG), retained profits (§ 34a EStG), foreign income and treaties. | Exact wording retained; current LF35, Who this applies to DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF84 | \| Gifts to clients \| In full if the year's gifts to that person cost EUR 50 or less; above that, nothing at all. Record each recipient \| | Exact wording retained; current LF84, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF85 | \| Rent of the home, home office \| Day rate EUR 6 per day worked mainly at home, max EUR 1,260. Room costs only if the room is the centre of all work \| | Exact wording retained; current LF85, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF86 | \| Fuel, car costs \| Depends on the car method; see below. No method confirmed: deduct nothing yet \| | Exact wording retained; current LF86, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF87 | \| Laptop, phone, furniture \| Over EUR 800 net: depreciate (computers and software: one year allowed). EUR 800 or less: expense now or pool \| | Exact wording retained; current LF87, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF88 | \| Health, care, pension insurance (Krankenkasse, DRV, KSK) \| Not a business expense. Special expense in Step 2 \| | Exact wording retained; current LF88, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF89 | \| Income tax, surcharge, church tax, prepayments \| Never a business expense (§ 12 no. 3 EStG). Church tax paid is a special expense in Step 2 (§ 10(1) no. 4 EStG) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF89, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF90 | \| Trade tax (Gewerbesteuer) \| Never deductible (§ 4(5b) EStG). Records that call it an expense are wrong for any year since 2008 \| | Exact wording retained; current LF90, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF91 | \| Fines from a court or authority \| Not deductible (§ 4(5) no. 8 EStG) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF91, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF92 | \| Transfers between own accounts, loan principal, private withdrawals \| Leave out. Interest on a business loan is an expense \| | Source-corrected ordinary gate; current LF92. Old unrestricted-interest or one-year carryback wording preserved here as corrected, not silently dropped. DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF93 | \| Wages, rent received, interest, dividends \| Not business income. Other forms (Anlage N, V, KAP) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF93, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF94 | \| Cash withdrawals \| Ask what the cash paid for. Default: leave out \| | Exact wording retained; current LF94, The taxable base DE-R06: statutory tax/Soli withdrawal priority/exclusion and qualifying outside-liability exception preserved in summary; detailed-regime referral retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF96 | **Cars.** A car used more than 50% for business can be a business asset: all costs are expenses and private use is added back at 1% of the gross list price per month (§ 6(1) no. 4 EStG), or by logbook. A private car used for business trips: EUR 0.30 per km or the actual cost share. Trips from home to the own office count only at EUR 0.38 per km one way. Electric and hybrid cars have special rules: refer. | Exact wording retained; current LF96, The taxable base DE-R01: business-car commuting positive-difference and actual/logbook method supplied in current Cars/Commuting section; separate private-use method retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF98 | **Phone and internet.** Deduct the business share shown by records. The employee flat rate from the wage tax guidelines is not a rule for the self-employed. | Exact wording retained; current LF102, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF100 | **Mixed and unknown items.** Default to no deduction until the client confirms the business share. | Exact wording retained; current LF104, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF102 | **Step 2: from profit to taxable income** (§ 2 EStG). Profit plus any other income = total income. Less special expenses: pension contributions within the cap; basic health and care cover in full; other insurance only if the cap has room; church tax paid, donations, or the EUR 36 lump sum. Less extraordinary burdens and child allowances (see de-employee-income-tax). Round down to whole euros. | Exact wording retained; current LF106, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF104 | **Step 3: tax.** Apply the tariff (or splitting). For a trader, subtract the § 35 credit: the lowest of 4.0 × Messbetrag, the tax that falls on trade income (positive trade income / all positive income × tariff tax), and the trade tax actually payable. Unused credit is lost. Then the surcharge on the tax after the credit, and church tax on the tax before the credit (§ 51a(2) EStG). Subtract prepayments paid. | Exact wording retained; current LF108, The taxable base  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF106 | **Losses.** A loss offsets other income of the same year, then goes back one year or forward without time limit (§ 10d EStG; see de-einkommensteuer-freelancer). | Source-corrected ordinary gate; current LF110. Old unrestricted-interest or one-year carryback wording preserved here as corrected, not silently dropped.  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF110 | \| Step \| Rule \| | Exact wording retained; current LF114, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF112 | \| Register the business \| Within one month of starting (§ 138 AO): a trade with the municipality, a Freiberufler with the tax office online \| | Exact wording retained; current LF116, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF113 | \| Forms \| Main form, Anlage S or G, Anlage EÜR with Anlage AVEÜR (one set per business), Anlage Vorsorgeaufwand, Anlage Sonderausgaben. Electronic, through ELSTER (§ 25(4) EStG, § 60(4) EStDV) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF117, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF114 | \| Own return \| Seven months after year end (§ 149(2) AO): 31 July 2027 is a Saturday, so Monday 2 August 2027 (§ 108(3) AO) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF118, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF115 | \| Prepared by a tax adviser \| Last day of February of the second year after (§ 149(3) AO): Tuesday 29 February 2028. The office can call for an earlier return with four months' notice (§ 149(4) AO) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF119, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF116 | \| Balance due \| One month after the notice (§ 36(4) EStG) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF120, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF117 | \| Prepayments \| 10 March, 10 June, 10 September, 10 December, set by notice from the last assessed tax. The office can adjust them until 15 months after year end; a rise after year end is due one month after the notice and only if at least EUR 5,000 (§ 37(3) to (5) EStG) \| | Exact wording retained; current LF121, Deadlines and filing  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF121 | - **Late-filing surcharge (§ 152 AO).** May be set for any late return. Must be set if the return is not in within 14 months after year end (for 2026: after 29 February 2028), unless the deadline was extended, the tax is nil, or the tax is not more than prepayments and tax withheld. Amount: 0.25% per started month of the tax less prepayments and tax withheld, at least EUR 25 per started month, at most EUR 25,000, rounded down to euros (§ 152(5) and (10) AO). | Exact wording retained; current LF125, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF122 | - **Interest (§ 233a AO).** From 15 months after year end (1 April 2028 for 2026) until the notice: 0.15% per full month, 1.8% a year (§ 238(1a) AO), on the balance rounded down to EUR 50. Refunds earn the same. | Exact wording retained; current LF126, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF123 | - **Late payment (§ 240 AO).** 1% per started month on the overdue amount rounded down to EUR 50. Applies to late prepayments too. | Exact wording retained; current LF127, Penalties and interest DE-R01: business-car commuting positive-difference and actual/logbook method supplied in current Cars/Commuting section; separate private-use method retained. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF124 | - **Wrong returns.** Tax evasion is a crime (§ 370 AO). Reckless understatement is an administrative offence with a fine of up to EUR 50,000 (§ 378 AO). Either case: refer. | Exact wording retained; current LF128, Penalties and interest  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF128 | - **Non-residents.** A person without a German home or habitual abode is taxed only on German income, including profit from a German permanent establishment or self-employed work done in Germany (§ 49 EStG). Use de-non-resident-tax. | Exact wording retained; current LF132, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF129 | - **Foreign clients.** Fees from foreign clients for work done in Germany by a resident are German income in full. VAT on cross-border services: germany-vat-return. | Exact wording retained; current LF133, Cross-border points  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF130 | - **Foreign tax, foreign establishment, moving in or out.** Foreign tax credits are in de-non-resident-tax (§ 34c EStG). A treaty may exempt profit of a foreign establishment, which then raises the rate (§ 32b EStG): refer. Departure with shares: leaving-germany-exit-tax. | DE-L09 source-corrected progression referral retained at current LF134: treaty exemption alone does not establish progression; actual32b exceptions must be checked. The foreign-credit and exit referrals are retained.  |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF131 | - **§ 7g abroad.** The investment deduction needs the asset used in a German establishment (§ 7g(1) EStG). | Exact wording retained; current LF135, Cross-border points  |
